Explain the relationship between quantization and memory requirements of the small transformer.

Answer: Quantization reduces the small transformer's memory requirements after training. The direction of the relationship is from quantization to a lower memory requirement for that trained model.

Actual score: 100.0

Relationship progress: 1 supported, 0 related, 0 contradicted. Green: independent understanding. Yellow: partial or assisted. Red: contradicted or not demonstrated. Complete: The learner correctly identifies that quantization reduces (inhibits) the memory requirements of the small transformer, satisfying the 'inhibits' relation defined in the fact. The phrase 'reduces' aligns with the definition 'reduces or suppresses'. The mention of 'after training' is a specific condition present in the learner's answer; since the supplied fact has no conditions (null), this additional detail does not contradict the fact but rather specifies when the effect occurs, which is permissible as it does not negate the core relationship. The direction (quantization -> lower memory) is正确

Explain the relationship between small transformer and self attention.

Answer: The small transformer uses self-attention to process text. Self-attention is a mechanism the model employs, rather than a separate model or the text itself.

Actual score: 100.0

Relationship progress: 1 supported, 0 related, 0 contradicted. Green: independent understanding. Yellow: partial or assisted. Red: contradicted or not demonstrated. Complete: The learner correctly identifies that the small transformer 'uses' self-attention as an employed mechanism to perform its function (processing text). Explain the relationship between small transformer and backpropagation.

Answer: During training, the small transformer uses backpropagation to improve its predictions. Backpropagation is the training method it employs for that improvement; the relationship is specifically about training.

Actual score: 100.0

Relationship progress: 1 supported, 0 related, 0 contradicted. Green: independent understanding. Yellow: partial or assisted. Red: contradicted or not demonstrated. Complete: The learner correctly identifies the source (small transformer), target (backpropagation), and condition (during training). The phrase 'uses backpropagation' accurately reflects the 'employs' relation defined in the fact. The mention of 'improve its predictions' aligns with the definition's purpose of performing a function, without inventing extra mechanisms.

Explain the relationship between regularization and small transformer.

Answer: Regularization helps the small transformer generalize beyond its training examples. It supports useful performance on examples beyond those used to train it, without guaranteeing that every prediction will be correct.

Actual score: 100.0

Relationship progress: 1 supported, 0 related, 0 contradicted. Green: independent understanding. Yellow: partial or assisted. Red: contradicted or not demonstrated. Complete: The learner correctly identifies the substantive connection ('helps') and specifies the direction/effect (facilitating generalization beyond training examples). The phrase 'generalize beyond its training examples' semantically satisfies the definition of facilitating or improving capability without needing to recite the exact rubric wording. The additional clause about not guaranteeing every prediction is consistent with the fact's modality constraints.

Answer: Self-attention processes text. In this relationship, self-attention is the mechanism doing the processing and text is the data it operates on.

maya:neurons and glia

Mode: normal

Explain the relationship between axon and electrical signal.

Answer: The axon carries an electrical signal along the neuron. Here the axon is the structure doing the carrying, and the electrical signal is what it conveys.

Actual score: 100.0

Relationship progress: 1 supported, 0 related, 0 contradicted. Green: independent understanding. Yellow: partial or assisted. Red: contradicted or not demonstrated. Explain the relationship between axon and terminal.

Answer: An axon may branch before reaching its terminals. The branching happens along the axon before those endpoints; the statement allows branching without saying every axon must branch.

Actual score: 100.0

Relationship progress: 1 supported, 0 related, 0 contradicted. Green: independent understanding. Yellow: partial or assisted. Red: contradicted or not demonstrated. elena:neurons and glia

Mode: normal

Explain the relationship between axon and electrical signal.

Answer: An axon conveys the neuron's electrical signal. This is a transport relationship: the axon is the conducting structure and the signal travels along it.

Actual score: 100.0

Relationship progress: 1 supported, 0 related, 0 contradicted. Green: independent understanding. Yellow: partial or assisted. Red: contradicted or not demonstrated. Explain the relationship between axon and terminal.

Answer: The axon can divide into branches before it reaches the terminals. The terminals are the endpoints reached after that possible branching, so this describes the axon's arrangement rather than requiring branching in every case.

Actual score: 100.0

Relationship progress: 1 supported, 0 related, 0 contradicted. Green: independent understanding. Yellow: partial or assisted. Red: contradicted or not demonstrated. The use of 'can divide' appropriately reflects the fact's 'may' modality without inventing a guaranteed outcome.
